# 1994 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** gray Mosaic · 14.4k · under-construction GIF · what’s cool today
**Star:** CSotD guestbook · `itt94-csotd` · `sites/csotd/index.html`
**ILS June:** 2,738 websites · 25,454,590 users
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 540
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt94-csotd`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1994/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1994/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1994 — thesis · 14.4 modem
2. Cool Site of the Day — visit + guestbook REAL
3. Yahoo! @ Stanford — browse, don't search
4. CERN / WWW · NCSA Mosaic
5. Fish Cam · White House
6. Year flow map — full trail tree

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt94-csotd`

**Room:** `years/1994/sites/csotd/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-csotd / guestbook`
**Nostalgia:** CSotD guestbook — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty name · skip guestbook · trap “this is Yahoo gold” never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt94-csotd`.

**Complete writes**
- Visit today’s cool site · sign guestbook (name ≥2) · honesty ticks · Save. Writes itt94-csotd.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1994` · key `itt94-csotd`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-csotd` — do not write `itt93-*` or `itt95-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Browse Yahoo · don't search → `years/1994/sites/yahoo/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 540 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt94-img-off` · pages/handbook.html

**Open:** `/years/1994/pages/handbook.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="img-off"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “pages handbook leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `cnn` · `bbc`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="img-off"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt94-img-off']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt94-csotd` stays empty. `itt93-img-off` and `itt95-img-off` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “pages handbook leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="img-off"]`.
- `localStorage['itt94-img-off']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1994", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt94-img-off`.
- Does **not** write `itt94-csotd`.
- **Next:** Starting Point → `/years/1994/pages/home.html`

**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="iuma-help"])`
**Need:** needPick=`keep`
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `cnn` · `bbc`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="iuma-help"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt94-iuma-help']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `keep`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
7. `itt94-csotd` stays empty. `itt93-iuma-help` and `itt95-iuma-help` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="keep"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="iuma-help"]`.
- `localStorage['itt94-iuma-help']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1994", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt94-iuma-help`.
- Does **not** write `itt94-csotd`.
- **Next:** IUMA listen → `/years/1994/sites/iuma/listen.html`

**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="personal-research"])`
**Need:** needPick=`keep`
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `cnn` · `bbc`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="personal-research"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt94-personal-research']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `keep`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
7. `itt94-csotd` stays empty. `itt93-personal-research` and `itt95-personal-research` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="keep"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="personal-research"]`.
- `localStorage['itt94-personal-research']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1994", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt94-personal-research`.
- Does **not** write `itt94-csotd`.
- **Next:** Prodigy → `/years/1994/sites/prodigy/more.html`
